# Reduction matrix

Generated by `node tools/matrix.js --md docs/REDUCTION-MATRIX.md`. Do not edit by hand: change `tools/matrix/features.js` or `tools/matrix/contexts.js` and run it again.

576 features × 7 binding-time contexts. Each cell is a tiny generated program, specialized by `js-frontend --js --why-json`, its residual run against the original in Node on 4 inputs (each in a fresh context, then all of them twice over in one loaded context), and its why report checked against what the context expects.

**MATRIX ok 3357 residual 662 wrong 0 crash 0 unsupported 7 n/a 6 (of 4032)**

## Legend

| mark | verdict | meaning |
|---|---|---|
| ✓ | ok | correct, and reduced as the context expects |
| R | residual | correct, but something is left that the context says should fold |
| W! | wrong | the residual disagrees with Node on some input: a specializer bug |
| C! | crash | the specializer panicked, died or timed out: a specializer bug |
| U | unsupported | the front end rejected the program |
| – | n/a | the context makes no sense for the feature |

Contexts (`F` is the feature's expression, `S`/`S2` known operands, `input` the runtime input):

- **static** — `return F(S)` with a known operand: main is `return <constant>`
- **branch** — `F(S)` in one arm of `if (input.flag)`, `F(S2)` in the other: only the branch is left (no gap, no merged, no call or new in main)
- **loop** — `var t = F(S); out.push(t)` in a loop over `input.list`: no more gap, merged, static or total units than the same loop with the constant written in
- **dynamic** — `return F(input.…)`: everything left depends on the input (no gap, no static unit; merged is reported, not failed)
- **helper** — `h(S) + "" + h(S2)` where `h(x)` returns `F(x)`: main is `return <constant>`
- **closure** — a closure over `var k = S` returning `F(k)`, called once: main is `return <constant>`
- **try** — `try { return F(S); } catch (e) { return "caught"; }`: main is `return <constant>`

Inputs: `{"n":3,"s":"ab","flag":true,"list":[1,2,3]}`, `{"n":0,"s":"","flag":false,"list":[]}`, `{"n":-7.5,"s":"Hello, World 42","flag":true,"list":[5]}`, `{"n":12,"s":"a1b22c","flag":false,"list":[4,3,2,1,0]}`.
